// scene.mjs MODE DIR [OUT]
//   preview DIR      the agent's check: render DIR, print a summary, save
//                    DIR/.preview/screenshot.png
//   check DIR OUT    the judged render: write OUT/checks.json,
//                    OUT/screenshot.png and OUT/shots/*.png. Reads optional
//                    OUT/input/check.json {modes: [...], mode_param: "tod"}.
// Both serve DIR on loopback with the site's scene policy (CSP, no outside
// requests) and render in Chromium with software WebGL (SwiftShader).
//
// check (version 2) records, per load: console/page errors, outside
// requests, missing files, frame rate, WebGL draw calls and triangles per
// frame, a blank test; plus a second screenshot 3 s later (animation), a
// second fresh load (determinism), one load per mode via ?<mode_param>=,
// and a click on each mode's button (does the scene change?).
import http from 'node:http';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { chromium } from 'playwright';
import { PNG } from 'pngjs';

const VERSION = 2;
const SETTLE_MS = 5000;
const [mode, dirArg, outArg] = process.argv.slice(2);
if (!['preview', 'check'].includes(mode) || !dirArg || (mode === 'check' && !outArg)) {
  console.error('usage: scene.mjs preview DIR | scene.mjs check DIR OUT');
  process.exit(2);
}
const root = path.resolve(dirArg);
const TYPES = {
  '.html': 'text/html', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.mjs': 'text/javascript',
  '.json': 'application/json', '.css': 'text/css', '.png': 'image/png',
  '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.glsl': 'text/plain',
  '.vert': 'text/plain', '.frag': 'text/plain', '.wasm': 'application/wasm',
};
// The policy the public site serves scenes with: same-origin files only.
const CSP = [
  "default-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval' blob:",
  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
  "img-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "connect-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "form-action 'none'",
  "frame-src 'none'",
].join('; ');

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://x');
  let file = path.join(root, decodeURIComponent(url.pathname));
  if (!file.startsWith(root)) { res.writeHead(403).end(); return; }
  if (fs.existsSync(file) && fs.statSync(file).isDirectory()) file = path.join(file, 'index.html');
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) { res.writeHead(404).end(); return; }
  res.writeHead(200, {
    'Content-Type': TYPES[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] || 'application/octet-stream',
    'Content-Security-Policy': CSP,
  });
  fs.createReadStream(file).pipe(res);
});
await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
const origin = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
const indexExists = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'index.html'));

// Counts draw calls and primitives per animation frame, page-side.
const GL_PROBE = () => {
  const stats = { frames: 0, draws: 0, prims: 0 };
  window.__wcGL = stats;
  const modeVerts = (m, n) => (m === 4 ? n / 3 : m === 5 || m === 6 ? Math.max(0, n - 2) : m === 1 ? n / 2 : n);
  for (const C of [window.WebGLRenderingContext, window.WebGL2RenderingContext]) {
    if (!C) continue;
    const p = C.prototype;
    for (const [fn, countArg, instArg] of [['drawArrays', 2, -1], ['drawElements', 1, -1], ['drawArraysInstanced', 2, 3], ['drawElementsInstanced', 1, 4]]) {
      const orig = p[fn];
      if (!orig) continue;
      p[fn] = function (...a) {
        stats.draws += 1;
        stats.prims += modeVerts(a[0], a[countArg]) * (instArg >= 0 ? a[instArg] : 1);
        return orig.apply(this, a);
      };
    }
  }
  const raf = window.requestAnimationFrame.bind(window);
  window.requestAnimationFrame = (cb) => raf((t) => { stats.frames += 1; cb(t); });
};

function analyse(png) {
  const img = PNG.sync.read(png);
  let n = 0, sum = 0, sum2 = 0; const colours = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < img.data.length; i += 4 * 7) {
    const [r, g, b] = [img.data[i], img.data[i + 1], img.data[i + 2]];
    const y = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
    n += 1; sum += y; sum2 += y * y;
    colours.add(((r >> 4) << 8) | ((g >> 4) << 4) | (b >> 4));
  }
  const luma_stddev = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sum2 / n - (sum / n) ** 2)) * 100) / 100;
  return { luma_stddev, distinct_colours: colours.size, blank: luma_stddev < 2 || colours.size < 4 };
}

// Fraction of sampled pixels that differ noticeably between two shots.
function diff(a, b) {
  const A = PNG.sync.read(a), B = PNG.sync.read(b);
  if (A.width !== B.width || A.height !== B.height) return 1;
  let n = 0, d = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < A.data.length; i += 4 * 5) {
    n += 1;
    if (Math.abs(A.data[i] - B.data[i]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 1] - B.data[i + 1]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 2] - B.data[i + 2]) > 16) d += 1;
  }
  return Math.round((d / n) * 10000) / 10000;
}

const browser = await chromium.launch({
  args: ['--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'],
});

// One fresh page load; returns its record and the screenshot.
async function load(query = '', { measure = true } = {}) {
  const r = { query, console_errors: [], console_warnings: [], page_errors: [], outside_requests: [], missing_files: [] };
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });
  await page.addInitScript(GL_PROBE);
  await page.route('**/*', (route) => {
    const u = route.request().url();
    if (u.startsWith(origin) || u.startsWith('data:') || u.startsWith('blob:')) return route.continue();
    r.outside_requests.push(u);
    return route.abort();
  });
  page.on('console', (m) => {
    if (m.type() === 'error') r.console_errors.push(m.text().slice(0, 500));
    if (m.type() === 'warning') r.console_warnings.push(m.text().slice(0, 300));
  });
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => r.page_errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 500)));
  page.on('response', (res) => { if (res.status() === 404) r.missing_files.push(res.url().replace(origin, '')); });
  if (indexExists) {
    await page.goto(`${origin}/index.html${query}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 60000 })
      .catch((e) => r.page_errors.push(`load: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 500)));
    await page.waitForTimeout(SETTLE_MS);
    if (measure) {
      Object.assign(r, await page.evaluate(() => new Promise((resolve) => {
        const s0 = { ...(window.__wcGL || {}) }; const t0 = performance.now();
        const done = () => {
          const s = window.__wcGL || {}; const secs = (performance.now() - t0) / 1000;
          const frames = (s.frames || 0) - (s0.frames || 0);
          resolve({
            fps: Math.round(frames / secs * 10) / 10,
            draw_calls_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.draws || 0) - (s0.draws || 0)) / frames) : null,
            primitives_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.prims || 0) - (s0.prims || 0)) / frames) : null,
            canvases: document.querySelectorAll('canvas').length,
          });
        };
        setTimeout(done, 2000);
      })).catch(() => ({ fps: null })));
    }
  }
  const png = await page.screenshot();
  Object.assign(r, analyse(png));
  return { r, png, page };
}

14: function mulberry32(seed) {
15:   let a = seed >>> 0;
16:   return function () {
17:     a |= 0;
18:     a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
19:     let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
20:     t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
21:     return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
22:   };
23: }

173: const outlineVertex = `
174:   uniform float uWidth;
175:   uniform float uPxScale;
176:   varying float vFogDepth;
177: 
178:   void main() {
179:     vec4 mvPosition = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
180:     vec3 viewNormal = normalize(normalMatrix * normal);
181:     float expand = uWidth * (-mvPosition.z) / uPxScale;
182:     mvPosition.xyz += viewNormal * expand;
183:     vFogDepth = -mvPosition.z;
184:     gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPosition;
185:   }
186: `;

190:   uniform vec3 uFogColor;

194:   void main() {
195:     float fogFactor = 1.0 - exp(-uFogDensity * uFogDensity * vFogDepth * vFogDepth);
196:     vec3 col = mix(uInk, uFogColor, fogFactor);
197:     gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
198:     #include <colorspace_fragment>
199:   }
200: `;

202: function outlineMaterial(widthPx) {
203:   const key = widthPx.toFixed(2);
204:   if (outlineCache.has(key)) return outlineCache.get(key);
205:   const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
206:     uniforms: {
207:       uWidth: { value: widthPx },
208:       uPxScale: outlinePxScale,
209:       uInk: outlineInk,
210:       uFogColor: outlineFogColor,
211:       uFogDensity: outlineFogDensity,
212:     },
213:     vertexShader: outlineVertex,
214:     fragmentShader: outlineFragment,
215:     side: THREE.BackSide,
216:     depthWrite: true,
217:     depthTest: true,
218:   });
219:   outlineCache.set(key, mat);
220:   return mat;
221: }

224: function addMesh(parent, geometry, material, opts = {}) {
225:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);

62: // Counts draw calls and primitives per animation frame, page-side.
63: const GL_PROBE = () => {
64:   const stats = { frames: 0, draws: 0, prims: 0 };
65:   window.__wcGL = stats;
66:   const modeVerts = (m, n) => (m === 4 ? n / 3 : m === 5 || m === 6 ? Math.max(0, n - 2) : m === 1 ? n / 2 : n);
67:   for (const C of [window.WebGLRenderingContext, window.WebGL2RenderingContext]) {
68:     if (!C) continue;
69:     const p = C.prototype;
70:     for (const [fn, countArg, instArg] of [['drawArrays', 2, -1], ['drawElements', 1, -1], ['drawArraysInstanced', 2, 3], ['drawElementsInstanced', 1, 4]]) {
71:       const orig = p[fn];
72:       if (!orig) continue;
73:       p[fn] = function (...a) {
74:         stats.draws += 1;
75:         stats.prims += modeVerts(a[0], a[countArg]) * (instArg >= 0 ? a[instArg] : 1);
76:         return orig.apply(this, a);
77:       };
78:     }
79:   }
80:   const raf = window.requestAnimationFrame.bind(window);
81:   window.requestAnimationFrame = (cb) => raf((t) => { stats.frames += 1; cb(t); });
82: };

84: function analyse(png) {
85:   const img = PNG.sync.read(png);
86:   let n = 0, sum = 0, sum2 = 0; const colours = new Set();
87:   for (let i = 0; i < img.data.length; i += 4 * 7) {
88:     const [r, g, b] = [img.data[i], img.data[i + 1], img.data[i + 2]];
89:     const y = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
90:     n += 1; sum += y; sum2 += y * y;
91:     colours.add(((r >> 4) << 8) | ((g >> 4) << 4) | (b >> 4));
92:   }
93:   const luma_stddev = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sum2 / n - (sum / n) ** 2)) * 100) / 100;
94:   return { luma_stddev, distinct_colours: colours.size, blank: luma_stddev < 2 || colours.size < 4 };
95: }
96: 
97: // Fraction of sampled pixels that differ noticeably between two shots.
98: function diff(a, b) {
99:   const A = PNG.sync.read(a), B = PNG.sync.read(b);
100:   if (A.width !== B.width || A.height !== B.height) return 1;
101:   let n = 0, d = 0;
102:   for (let i = 0; i < A.data.length; i += 4 * 5) {
103:     n += 1;
104:     if (Math.abs(A.data[i] - B.data[i]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 1] - B.data[i + 1]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 2] - B.data[i + 2]) > 16) d += 1;
105:   }
106:   return Math.round((d / n) * 10000) / 10000;
107: }

909: function towerWidth(y) {
910:   if (y <= H1) {
911:     const t = clamp(y / H1, 0, 1);
912:     return 25.5 + 37.0 * Math.pow(1 - t, 1.35);
913:   }
914:   if (y <= H2) {
915:     const t = (y - H1) / (H2 - H1);
916:     return 25.5 + (19.5 - 25.5) * Math.pow(t, 0.7);
917:   }
918:   if (y <= H3) {
919:     const t = (y - H2) / (H3 - H2);
920:     return 19.5 + (8.8 - 19.5) * Math.pow(t, 0.65);
921:   }
922:   const t = clamp((y - H3) / (TOP - H3), 0, 1);
923:   return 8.8 + (3.4 - 8.8) * t;
924: }

931: function boxGeometry(w, h, d, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0) {
932:   const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
933:   g.translate(x, y, z);
934:   return g;
935: }

937: const UP = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
938: function beamGeometry(a, b, thickness) {
939:   const dirv = b.clone().sub(a);
940:   const len = dirv.length();
941:   if (len < 0.05) return null;
942:   const mid = a.clone().add(b).multiplyScalar(0.5);
943:   const q = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(UP, dirv.normalize());
944:   const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(thickness, len, thickness);
945:   const m = new THREE.Matrix4().compose(mid, q, new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1));
946:   g.applyMatrix4(m);
947:   return g;
948: }
949: 
950: function pushBeam(arr, a, b, thickness) {
951:   const g = beamGeometry(a, b, thickness);
952:   if (g) arr.push(g);
953: }

955: function cornerPoint(y, sx, sz) {
956:   const w = towerWidth(y);
957:   return new THREE.Vector3(sx * w, y, sz * w);
958: }

960: function faceCorners(y, face) {
961:   const w = towerWidth(y);
962:   if (face === 0) return [new THREE.Vector3(-w, y, -w), new THREE.Vector3(w, y, -w)];
963:   if (face === 1) return [new THREE.Vector3(w, y, -w), new THREE.Vector3(w, y, w)];
964:   if (face === 2) return [new THREE.Vector3(w, y, w), new THREE.Vector3(-w, y, w)];
965:   return [new THREE.Vector3(-w, y, w), new THREE.Vector3(-w, y, -w)];
966: }

968: function buildTowerLattice() {

1067: function buildArch(face) {
1068:   const y0 = 12;
1069:   const apex = 44;
1070:   const w0 = towerWidth(y0);
1071:   const pts = [];
1072:   for (let i = 0; i <= 12; i++) {
1073:     const s = i / 12;
1074:     const u = s * 2 - 1;
1075:     let x, z;
1076:     if (face === 0) { x = u * w0 * 0.97; z = -towerWidth(y0 + (apex - y0) * (1 - u * u)); }
1077:     else if (face === 1) { x = towerWidth(y0 + (apex - y0) * (1 - u * u)); z = u * w0 * 0.97; }
1078:     else if (face === 2) { x = u * w0 * 0.97; z = towerWidth(y0 + (apex - y0) * (1 - u * u)); }
1079:     else { x = -towerWidth(y0 + (apex - y0) * (1 - u * u)); z = u * w0 * 0.97; }
1080:     pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, y0 + (apex - y0) * (1 - u * u), z));
1081:   }
1082:   const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts, false, 'centripetal', 0.5);
1083:   const tube = new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 28, 0.78, 6, false);
1084:   towerBeamGeos.push(tube);
1085: 
1086:   // little spandrel braces above the arch
1087:   for (let i = 2; i <= 10; i += 2) {
1088:     const p = pts[i];
1089:     const up = new THREE.Vector3(
1090:       face === 1 || face === 3 ? towerWidth(H1) : p.x,
1091:       H1,
1092:       face === 0 || face === 2 ? towerWidth(H1) : p.z
1093:     );
1094:     pushBeam(towerBeamGeos, p, up, 0.18);

class Timer {

	/**
	 * Constructs a new timer.
	 */
	constructor() {

		this._previousTime = 0;
		this._currentTime = 0;
		this._startTime = performance.now();

		this._delta = 0;
		this._elapsed = 0;

		this._timescale = 1;

		this._document = null;
		this._pageVisibilityHandler = null;

	}

	/**
	 * Connect the timer to the given document.Calling this method is not mandatory to
	 * use the timer but enables the usage of the Page Visibility API to avoid large time
	 * delta values.
	 *
	 * @param {Document} document - The document.
	 */
	connect( document ) {

		this._document = document;

		// use Page Visibility API to avoid large time delta values

		if ( document.hidden !== undefined ) {

			this._pageVisibilityHandler = handleVisibilityChange.bind( this );

			document.addEventListener( 'visibilitychange', this._pageVisibilityHandler, false );

		}

	}

	/**
	 * Disconnects the timer from the DOM and also disables the usage of the Page Visibility API.
	 */
	disconnect() {

		if ( this._pageVisibilityHandler !== null ) {

			this._document.removeEventListener( 'visibilitychange', this._pageVisibilityHandler );
			this._pageVisibilityHandler = null;

		}

		this._document = null;

	}

	/**
	 * Returns the time delta in seconds.
	 *
	 * @return {number} The time delta in second.
	 */
	getDelta() {

		return this._delta / 1000;

	}

	/**
	 * Returns the elapsed time in seconds.
	 *
	 * @return {number} The elapsed time in second.
	 */
	getElapsed() {

		return this._elapsed / 1000;

	}

	 * Returns the timescale.
	 *
	 * @return {number} The timescale.
